2. RECOGNITION 
A.   INTRODUCTION:    Student Representatives to the Board 
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Introduce Bria and Andrew. 
Bria Ellis, Athens High School 
Bria is a senior at Athens High School and is participating in 
Student Congress for the first time.  She has been active in other 
clubs at Athens, including the Literary Club  and Invisible 
Children.  Bria also plays women’s lacrosse at Athens.  
Andrew Michail, Troy High School 
This year we welcome back Andrew Michail , who ser ved as the 
student representative from Troy High last year.  Andrew is 
currently serving as junior class president at Troy High School.   
He is a very active member of Troy High’s Student Government 
and will be serving as a committee chair for multiple even ts this 
year.

5. B. 09.21.10 
BOARD OF EDUCATION 
TROY, MICHIGAN 
BOARD WORKSHOP   MINUTES  SEPTEMBER 14, 2010 
    ADMINISTRATION BUILDING 
Vice-President Miletti called the workshop to order at 6:00 p.m.  In addition to M r. 
Miletti, the following Board members were present:  Mrs. Edmunds, Mr. Hauff,  Ms. 
Philippart, and Mrs. Pochodylo.  Ms. Underwood and Mrs. Fleming were absent.  
Administrators in attendance were Dr. Fowler, Ms. Birmingham, Mrs. Keeling, Dr. 
Machesky, Mr. Rajter, Mr. Hosang, and Mrs. Buchanan, Assistant Principal  at Smith 
Middle School.  Rhonda Hendrickson, President of Friends of the Troy Public Library, was 
in attendance for her presentation/discussion . One additional member of the 
community was also in attendance.  The following items were discussed: 
1. Public Communication* 
2. Grading Pilot – Smith Middle School 
3. Staffing/Enrollment/Schools of Choice Updates 
4. School Funding/Financial Update 
5. Sale of District Property 
6. MASB Fall Conference (November 4 – 7, 2010) – Delegates and Alternates for 
 2010 Delegate Assembly 
7. Library Millage Proposal 
8. Other 
9. Adjournment 
The meeting was adjourned at 8:49 p.m. 
 Respectfully submitted, 
 Nancy L. Philippart, Secretary

  6. A.    09.21.10 
6. BOARD NEGOTIATING TEAM 
A. CONSIDERATION:  Appointment of Board Negotiating Teams for 
TEA and TESA Contract Negotiations 
 The collective bargaining agreement between the Troy School District 
and the TEA/MEA/NEA expires on August  14, 20 11.  This union 
represents the teaching staff in the Troy School District.   
 The collective bargaining agreement between the Troy School District 
and the TESA/MEA/NEA expires on June 30, 20 11.  This union 
represents the secretarial staff in the Troy School District.   
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Accept the resolution below. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
  Moved by  
  Seconded by 
Be it therefore resolved  that the Troy Board of Education hereby appoints 
Jasen Witt  as its chief spokesperson and Mark Raj ter, Jan Keeling  and 
Richard Machesky  as team members to represent the Board in upcoming 
negotiations with the TEA/MEA/NEA, whose contract expires on August 14, 
2011.   
Be it further resolved  that the Troy Board of Education hereby appoints 
Jasen Witt as its chief spokesperson and Mark Rajter, Richard Machesky and 
Bonnie Lobert  as team members to represent the Board in upcoming 
negotiations with the TESA/MEA/NEA, whose contract expires on June 30, 
2011.   
Be it further resolved that the Board desires to reach a fair and equitable 
agreement with each of the Associations. 
 Ayes 
 Nays 
85
 7. A.   09.21.10 
7. CURRICULUM 
A. REPORT:  Professional Development for 20 10/2011 School 
Year 
During spring 20 10, planning began for the professional 
development activities for the 2010 /2011 school year .  
Tonight, we are pleased to share several professional 
learning opportunities that occurred and how they will be 
sustained to support the professional learning of teachers 
and administrators in the Troy School District.  
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  This evening’s report is presented  by 
Mrs. Anne Mull, Troy School District’s Director of Instruction and 
Assessment.

 Atlas Rubicon began the use of this system to store and 
share curriculum documents
 Secondary math curriculum work - begin the work of 
aligning to the common core
 Elementary reading curriculum work

10.  OTHER 
 A. CONSIDERATION:      Certification of Troy School District Board 
Delegates and Alternates to the Michigan Association of School 
Boards’ 2010 Delegate Assembly 
  Delegates and alternates must be certified  by the Board of 
Education to be its representatives, allowing them to make 
motions and vote on issues at the MASB 20 10 Delegate Assembly 
during the MASB Fall Conference in November. 
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Adopt the following resolution. 
   Moved by  
   Supported by  
Be It Therefore Resolved  that the Troy Board of Education hereby certifies  
Mrs. Ida Edmunds, Mrs. Paula Fleming, and Ms. Nancy Philippart as delegates 
to the MASB 2010 Delegate Assembly on November 4, 2010. 
Ayes:  
Nayes:  
I certify that the above resolution was adopted at the Troy Board of 
Education meeting on Tuesday, September 21, 2010. 
      Nancy L. Philippart, Secretary 
      Troy Board of Education

11.  CLOSED SESSION 
A. CONSIDERATION:  Negotiations 
The Board must pass a resolution to go into closed session. 
Following the passage of the resolution, the Board of Education will 
go into closed session.  No additional business will take place after 
this time, other than to officially adjourn the meeting. 
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Adopt the following resolution. 
   Moved by  
   Supported by  
Be it therefore resolved that, as permitted in the Michigan Open Meetings Act, 
Public Act 267, Section 8 (c), the Troy Board of Education shall go into closed 
session to discuss negotiations.
